<meta>This page continues the discussion from page 70 regarding the origin of the will. It transitions from the general principle of the "Word of Revelation" to the specific historical and spiritual moment of the Fall of both the devil and humanity, focusing on the concept of "Schiedlichkeit" (distinction/separability).</meta>

### Chapter 6: The Election of Grace
<note>original: *Gnaden-Wahl*; This refers to the theological concept of predestination or how God chooses those who receive grace.</note>

33. Understand it thus: That same **Soul-Knowledge** <term>Scienz</term> <note>From the Latin *scientia*; Böhme uses this to mean a living, active knowledge or a conscious spiritual power</note> gazed longingly at the creation of the **formed Word** in its **distinction**, and knew within itself that same power for diversity, and arose in a desire for **distinction**: immediately, distinction became manifest in the creature according to both soul and body; however, the devil was the primary cause of this.

34. For since he, as a fiery spirit, had departed from the **Balance** <term>Temperatur</term> <note>The state where all qualities are in perfect harmony; the divine equilibrium</note> and from the Image of God, he then also led his desire into the Soul-Knowledge of man, to introduce it into a lustful longing. For he well noticed what Adam was—namely, a **Throne-Prince** <note>original: *Thron-Fürste*; a high-ranking angelic or spiritual ruler</note> in his former seat in the Kingdom of God. But he did not know the name **JESUS**, or that this name would manifest itself in man within time; for his knowledge of God’s love—in which the name Jesus is the highest sweetness of the Godhead—had died in his fall. That is to say, it had **transmuted** <note>original: *transmutiret*; changed from one substance or state to another</note> itself into malice; therefore, he now knew only malice.

35. Thus one now understands the ground and beginning of the fall of the devil and of man: not that one can say God willed it, insofar as He is God; rather, the **distinction** <term>Schiedlichkeit</term> <note>A key term for Böhme referring to the capacity of the unified divine power to separate into distinct, individual qualities or "shards"</note> from nature into the creature willed it, and that distinction is not called "God."

36. God leads His unique will into the forming and grasping of His Word toward distinction, as if toward the revelation of God: there, distinction stands in **free will**, for distinction is nature, and it is also the creation. And within distinction, God wills both evil and good: as in that which has separated itself into the good—such as the holy angels—there He wills good within them; and in that which has separated itself into the evil—such as the devils—there He wills evil within them, just as the Scripture says: *Whatever kind of people they are, such a God they also have; with the holy you are holy, and with the perverse you are perverse.* **Psalm 18:26-27.**

37. Now Reason speaks: If God, in His flowed-forth, formed Word, is Himself everything—such as evil and good, life and death—then wherein lies the human struggle? Why does one contend for the sake of God’s will, since God in His formed Word is everything, and also wills everything, be it evil or good, each in its property from which it **originally** <term>urständlich</term> <note>Relating to the "Urstand" or the primordial, original state of being</note> emerged?

E 4
<meta>catchword: 38. See,</meta>

<summary>Böhme explains that the Fall resulted from the soul's desire to experience "distinction" rather than divine unity, emphasizing that while God contains all possibilities, the creature's free will determines whether they experience His holy or "perverse" aspect.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of Chapter 6, "The Election of Grace," specifically addressing the origin of evil and the "hardening" of hearts. It moves from the historical fall of the devil (on page 71) to a philosophical explanation of how human reason misunderstands God's nature and the role of "distinction" in creation.</meta>

# XV. Concerning Chapter 6.

38. See, therein lies the conflict: that reason, in its conceit and without Divine Light, is a fool before GOD and does not know what GOD is. It always imagines Him as something foreign and distant when it wishes to speak of GOD. In the eternal, unchangeable God—in His eternal Trinity—reason creates a beginning will and purpose, yet understands nothing of how all beginnings and purposes originate in the **formed Word** <term>formed Word</term> <note>original: *geformten Worte*; Böhme’s term for the divine creative power as it expresses itself into the physical and spiritual world of nature.</note> through Nature. It is there that the Word conceives and forms itself into Nature, and all beginnings lie within the forming of the Word, as in the Creation. This occurs within the great <term>Mystery</term> <note>original: *Mysterio*</note> of <term>Distinction</term> <term>Schiedlichkeit</term> <note>original: *Schiedlichkeit*; a central concept for Böhme meaning the "separability" or "differentiation" of the unified divine power into many distinct qualities.</note>, in which the creatures originate. Thus, all evil comes from Nature and the creature, and the **hardening** <term>Verstockung</term> <note>The process by which a soul becomes rigid and closed off from divine grace.</note> originates in Nature and the creature, specifically in the <term>Scienz</term> <note>original: *Scienz*; from the Latin *scientia*, meaning a living, active spiritual knowledge or self-awareness.</note> of the creaturely selfhood. When the creature turns itself into the wrath of Nature, it is seized and hardened therein; it conceives and holds the "speaking" within wrath. And all this (when it is written:

> He hardens their hearts, so that they do not believe and are not saved

) happens in the formed Word of eternal and temporal Nature.

39. For GOD also speaks from this source when He says in the Psalm of David:

> You shall see and have your delight in it, how the ungodly are repaid. Ps. 91: 8. Likewise: you shall rejoice when the ungodly is cast down; that is, when the ungodly is swallowed up in wrath, which was an opposite and a constant poisoning to the Saint. Ps. 64: 8. <note>In Böhme’s theology, "poison" often refers to the harsh, conflicting qualities of nature when they are not tempered by divine love.</note>

When that same poison is taken away from the Holy Soul, it rejoices that it is delivered from distress. And for this reason, the Word also stands in the pain of Nature, so that joy may be made manifest; but the **Distinction** <term>Schiedlichkeit</term> proceeds from the Word without compulsion, in free will, each quality into its own. For in the Holy Word everything is good, but in the introduction of a Self-Will, it becomes evil.

40. This happens now in Nature and the creature, and not at all in GOD; otherwise, the devil’s will would also have to be in the Word of GOD, if GOD drove all things into an inevitability within His Word. But the devil’s will, as well as Adam’s sinful will, arose in its own **Scienz** <term>Scienz</term> within the creature, and not in GOD. Rather, in the Center of Nature, the individual **Scienz** conceived itself into a will of **Pride**,

<meta>catchword: wanting</meta>

<summary>Böhme argues that evil and the "hardening of hearts" originate within the creature's own self-will and the "distinction" of Nature, rather than within God's essential being. He explains that joy can only be known in contrast to the "pain" of nature, but the choice to turn toward wrath belongs to the creature alone.</summary>

->### Chapter 6. The Election of Grace.<-

...wishing to be equal to, or even more than, the speaking Word in the Trinity of the Deity; humility was despised and abandoned, and in its place, the **power of fire** was embraced.

41. This is the Fall: that Adam and Lucifer placed **Fantasy** <term>Phantasey</term> <note>original: *Phantasey*; for Böhme, this is not mere daydreaming, but a false, self-centered reality that replaces divine truth.</note> in God's stead. At that, the Holy Spirit departed from their nature. Now they are a spirit within their own will and are imprisoned in Fantasy, just as we recognize this in Adam. When the soul's **Knowing** <note>original: *Sciens*; referring to the soul's active self-awareness or spiritual knowledge.</note> rose up through the devil’s urging or **infection** <note>original: *Inficiren*</note>, the Holy Spirit withdrew into His own **Principle** <term>Principium</term>. Then Adam, who was once in the image of God, became faint and weak in his **Temperament** <term>Temperatur</term> <note>original: *Temperatur*; the state of perfect balance where all spiritual properties are in harmony.</note>. He could no longer magically bring forth his own kind in that same equality; his omnipotence, which he possessed while in the *Temperament*, was broken, for the animal qualities of **Creation** began to stir within him.

42. Thus **Moses** says: God caused a deep sleep to fall upon him, and he fell asleep. *Genesis 2:21.* Here, he has now fallen asleep to the *Temperament* (meaning, to the Divine World). From this sleep, **Christ** must now awaken him, or else he can no longer see God within the creature. For this falling asleep was nothing other than losing God’s light in Love (as the **Fire of Love**); that light was extinguished in the **Essence** <note>original: *Ens*</note> of the heavenly world’s being. Thus, he was already half dead.

43. The length of time that Adam stood in the true image of God is presented to you in the **figures of Moses and Christ** <note>original: *Figuren Mosis und Christi*; symbolic patterns or "types" found in the Bible.</note>, as is the time of his sleep. If you have the sight to see, place Moses in the figure of Christ, and Christ in the figure of Adam, as when Adam stood in innocence.

44. Moses was on the mountain for forty days while Israel was **tested** <note>original: *probiret*</note>; Israel was in the wilderness for forty years; and Christ stood for forty days in Adam’s **Trial** <term>Proba</term> <note>original: *Proba*; a period of testing or proof of one's spiritual integrity.</note> during the temptation in the wilderness. Furthermore, He walked for forty days after His resurrection in the true, perfected *Trial* in which Adam should have walked in his innocence, before his confirmation toward the magical birth. But because this could not be (which was indeed well known in God), Adam fell into sleep. Therefore, Christ later had to rest for forty hours in Adam’s sleep <note>Referring to the time Christ spent in the tomb.</note> and awaken Adam within Himself back into the Kingdom of God. Ponder this, and you will learn to understand the entire foundation of the **Process of Christ** <term>Proceß Christi</term>. Simply place Christ in Adam’s stead, and you will find

## XX. Concerning Chapter 6.

...you have the entire foundation of the Old and New Testaments; place Adam in the formed word of Creation, and let him be the image of the outer and inner eternal nature of all three principles: and place Christ in the eternally-speaking Word, according to the true Divine property, wherein no evil may arise, but only the birth of love of Divine Revelation according to the Kingdom of Glory; and lead Christ into Adam, so that Christ may give new birth to Adam within himself, and **tincture** <note>original: *tingire*; to permeate or transform something spiritually, much like an alchemical dye.</note> him with love, so that he may awaken from the deep sleep; thus you have the entire process of Adam and Christ.

45. For Adam is the spoken, formed, creaturely word, and Christ is the holy, eternally-speaking Word: Thus you will lead time into eternity, and will see more than you may learn in all the books of men.

46. For when Eve was made out of Adam in Adam’s sleep, it happened in the **Word "Let there be"** <note>original: *Verbo Fiat*</note>, in the **Spirit of the World** <note>original: *Spiritu Mundi*</note>; there they became creatures of the outer world, formed into the outer natural life into mortality, as into the animal life, with beast-like members in their form, and also with a **sack of maggots** <note>original: *Madenſack*; a vivid term used by Böhme to describe the corruptible earthly body and digestive system.</note> for earthly food. For after the woman came out of Adam, the image of God in the **perfect balance** <term>Temperatur</term> <note>original: *Temperatur*; the state of harmony where no single element or quality dominates.</note> was already broken, and Paradise could not persist therein; for the Kingdom of God does not consist in eating and drinking, as the Scripture says, but in peace and joy in the Holy Spirit (Romans 14:17). That could not be in Adam and Eve, for they already bore the sign of the animal kind; although the animal kind was not yet fully awakened, it was already awakened in desire.

47. The Tree of Temptation—of the knowledge of Good and Evil—was precisely the **test** <note>original: *Proba*</note>, toward which the human, soulful **knowing** <note>original: *Scienz*; from the Latin *scientia*, referring here to the soul's active consciousness or self-awareness.</note> (out of the will of the **Abyss** <term>Ungrund</term> <note>original: *Ungrund*; the "Groundless," the primal nothingness or absolute freedom from which God and all existence emerge.</note>) would want to turn; whether it wished to remain standing in the creature, in the perfect balance; or whether it wished to turn itself into the Spirit of the World, into the divided properties?

48. Thus Reason speaks: Why did God let it grow? Answer: Hear, Reason: the **test** <note>original: *Proba*</note> of this world is better than the center in the fire according to the right of eternity to...

# Chapter 6. The Election of Grace.

...be tested, just as Lucifer was tested; God also truly recognized the Fall of man within the **Spirit of the World** <note>original: *Spiritu Mundi*; the animating breath or soul of the physical cosmos.</note>, for what the **Science** <note>original: *Scienz*; in Böhme’s terminology, this refers to the soul’s inherent "knowing" power or its magnetic attraction toward a specific essence.</note> of the soul desired, the Earth had to provide; for its lust went into the property of the earth. Thus, the earth had to present to that lust whatever it wanted: for the **Science** of the soul belongs to the Divine property according to Omnipotence. And within this lies the foundation of all mystery, and the Fall remains the same now as then, based on man's own will and the devil's deception.

49. The true and actual Fall of man is this: When Eve was made out of Adam, the devil placed himself within the serpent and lay by the Tree of Temptation. He persuaded Eve that she should eat from it, for then her eyes would be opened and she would be like God; she would know what existed in all properties, and what kind of **Essence** <note>original: *Ens*; the core substance or being of a thing.</note> and taste was within them—how all powers tasted in their specific properties, and what all animals were in their natures. All of which was indeed true; but the devil did not tell her about her naked form, or how heat and cold would pierce her. Nor did he come in his own form, but in the form of the craftiest animal. Furthermore, the devil's goal was to make Eve—as the **Matrix** <note>original: *Matricem*; the generative womb or source.</note> in the **Venus Tincture** <note>original: *Veneris Tinctur*; the spiritual quality of beauty, desire, and love.</note>—monstrous, so that she would be captivated by the serpent's craftiness. From this arose the lust to know good and evil, as it existed in the serpent's cunning, where the **Science** of nature had led itself into a **Phantasy** <note>original: *Phantasey*; a delusive, self-centered imagination or false reality.</note> of such craftiness. It is not as Reason suggests, that God armed the serpent's tongue so that it *had* to do this: one could well say that the devil armed it from the kingdom of **Phantasy**, but one cannot say that of God.

50. The serpent was an **Essence** <note>original: *Ens*</note> in the three First Principles (namely in **Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury** <note>original: *Sal, Sulphur und Mercurio*; the three fundamental alchemical forces Böhme uses to describe the foundation of all matter and spirit.</note>) within natural **Science**, where fire and light divide, and where understanding still lies within a fiery sharpness. For the spirit of understanding is not yet separated from the **Center** of the three First Principles, but is instead mixed with pain, as if with the root of poisonous torment. Therefore, within the serpent lies the highest cause of poison and the false, cunning will; and yet, within it also lies the highest **Preservation** <note>original: *Præservation*; a protective remedy or antidote.</note> against poison, if from it—

He describes the serpent's nature using alchemical principles, noting it contains both the source of poison and its own remedy.</summary>

# XV. Regarding Chapter 6.

...the poison is separated, as is to be thought of concerning *Lucifer* and his followers.

51. Satan was also of the highest fiery **Science** <term>Science (*Scienz*): In Böhme's specialized vocabulary, this refers to the root-will or the soul’s innate power of "knowing" and attraction; a magnetic power of the spirit.</term>, according to the realm of nature, and one of the most beautiful in heaven, for which the fiery **Science** of nature was a cause of his shining glory: he had taken upon himself both the worst and the best; understand, the eternal **Science** had taken upon itself the fiery nature, according to the highest mobility (from which strength and power consist or arise), in which the **Science** of the **Abyss** <term>Abyss (*Ungrund*): The groundless, primordial source of all being that exists before and beyond nature.</term> had then formed itself in its own will, after the manner of craftiness, and broke away from humility, desiring to rule in the light of God, in His mirror, in all powers, as he indeed did in his beginning; whereby he poisoned the essence in the **Science** of nature with such a quality, from which poisoned **Essence** <note>original: *Ens*; the core spiritual substance or "being" of a thing.</note> the serpent also took its origin in the creation: on account of which poisoning God also cursed the earth, after man had poisoned it even more with the devil’s poison and craftiness through his introduced false lust, with which he poisoned the **Science** in the essence from which he had been drawn out, so that **Paradise** <note>original: *Paradeis*</note> withdrew from him.

52. Thus the devil also placed his poisoned essence, with the serpent, at the tree, wherein he had—prior to the times of the creation of the earth—introduced his discharge <note>original: *Egest*; a term for the "outgoing" or "excrement" of a spiritual desire or will.</note> and crafty will into the **Science** of nature and its spiritual essence; which essence in the **Science** of nature, in the beginning of the creation <note>original: *Creation*</note>, also entered into the **Creature** <note>original: *Creatur*</note>, as is to be considered in all poisonous worms. Not that the devil created them: No, but he was only a poisoner of nature, in the manner that he poisoned his own nature as well as human nature. But the **Fiat** <note>original: *Fiat*; Latin for "Let it be," referring to the creative command of God that gives form to matter.</note> made them, each property of the divided **Science** into its like form: as the will in the **Science** was in the working figure, so also the **Creature** became.

53. For the speaking Word in every property of **Science** led itself into an image; thus the serpent was close to the devil in the **Science** of nature, for he had cast his poisonous will into it when it was not yet a worm. Yet, so that one maintains a distinction between earthly creatures

And when she ate, and did not immediately fall down and die, she gave to Adam also, and he also ate of it; for Adam had already dipped into it while he stood in the image of God, but had not yet eaten into the body until this moment.

# XV. From Chapter 7.

...ship sits, 4. and all the properties of Nature penetrate into him. 5. Through the bite <note>A reference to eating the forbidden fruit.</note> he became an animal of all animals according to the outward body, only with a nobler **Science** <term>Science (*Scienz*): The soul’s innate "knowing-power" or magnetic attraction that directs its will.</term>, 6. and in that hour God’s wrath, hell, and the realm of **Fantasy** <note>In Böhme’s view, "Fantasy" refers to a state of delusion or false imagination divorced from divine reality.</note> became manifest in him, 7. 8. so that the Divine Image in the soul faded, in the same way that the night swallows the day into itself. 9. Just as if the sun were to vanish, an eternal night would follow and nothing could grow: so Adam and Eve died to the **Light-Power**. 10. Not that the soul’s **Science** had died: but the Spirit of God hid Himself, 11. because the **Science** of the free will withdrew itself from Him. 12. Nevertheless, God dwells through all things; but nothing grasps Him. 13. Thus the soul became as night, wherein the senses always swing themselves upward in the animal will, desiring only to be the most beautiful child. 14. Into this faded image, the Word of God's Love spoke itself again, 15. and here the **Providence** <note>original: *Versehung*</note> is to be understood. For after God had seen the Fall in the fire of nature, He provided the Holy Name **JESUS** with the highest **Love-Essence** <term>Essence (*Ens*): The fundamental spiritual substance or "being" of a thing.</term> therein. 16. The Devil’s "speaking-in" happened in Adam, and the Divine Speaking pressed into Eve, as into the **Matrix** <term>Matrix: Latin for "womb" or "source-point."</term> and **Mother** <note>original: *Gebärerin*</note> of all human beings. 17. 18. Christ’s coming (John 3:13) must be understood in terms of **Being** <note>original: *Wesen*</note>. 19. The Word need only move itself; it is present everywhere. <note>original: *ibid.* (in the same place)</note> For the speaking or creating Word was meant to produce Adam; 20. so the making and the made Word must do it. <note>original: *ibid.*</note> The inner Word took the faded **Essence** into its living one and made it alive. 21. Since the covenant now presses upon everyone, where then does God’s will for the **hardening** <term>Hardening (*Verstockung*): The spiritual state of being closed off or calloused against God’s grace.</term> of man originate? 22. From the **Essence** introduced by the Devil, no soul could or should be born: for Adam and Eve were **One Man** in the Word, and upon that same one, grace pressed. 23. Where now is the Divine Purpose? 24. 25. How is the ground of hardening to be understood? 26. It is true that after the Fall, all possibility for good was gone, according to body and soul: nevertheless, the **Science** remained free, for it is the unique Will of God, 27. which Will is the Eternal Beginning of Divine Wisdom. 28. For Love cannot become manifest without the Wrath; thus the root of the **Science** is the **Wrath-Fire**. 29. When the light in the soul went out, it was a **source of torment** <note>original: *Quall*. A Böhmean pun on *Qual* (torment) and *Quelle* (source/wellspring).</note> of God’s wrath, into which God’s love embodied itself; and in this embodiment, man is provided for in Christ Jesus. 30. From eternity, the name **JESUS** has stood in an immovable love within man. 31. ### XV. Regarding Chapter 7.

1. The foundation of how all beings originate from a single source, and how all creatures are contained within the *Spirit of the World* <note>original: *Spiritu Mundi*</note>, as in the spoken Word of God. For this reason, we can rightly say that all creatures were also contained within Adam—not that they physically proceeded from Adam and entered into the creation; but rather, they were in the eternal science of the soul. In this science, the Word of God forms and shapes itself into a natural and created foundation. Within this, all properties are understood, as Moses testifies: that man was intended to rule over all creatures; but now, after the Fall, they rule over him.

2. For when the soul stood within the balanced harmony <term>Temperature (*Temperatur*): In Böhme's philosophy, this refers to the perfect state of balance between the opposing qualities of nature (like heat and cold, or light and dark), where no single element dominates.</term>, the soul's will-spirit penetrated through all creatures and was harmed by none, for none could grasp it. Just as no creature can grasp the sun’s power and light with its own will, but must allow it to penetrate through; so it was also with the will-spirit of man. But when he was captured in the serpent's poison, within the will of the devil, he became an enemy to all creatures and lost this power.

3. Furthermore, the creatures gained power within him and rose up in him, as is now plain to see: for one person possesses the property of a cunning serpent, full of wicked craftiness and poisonous malice; another has the property of a toad; another of a dog, a cat, a basilisk, a lion, a bear, a wolf, and so on, through all the properties of animals and worms.

4. Outwardly, they indeed possess the first-formed human image, but within that property sits an evil beast. The same is to be understood regarding good, tame animals; for some people possess the nature of a good animal's property. Indeed, there is no human conceived from Adam's seed who does not carry the property of some animal within their earthly body—some an evil one, some a good one.

5. This is now understood through the Fall: that all properties in the *Spirit of the World* <note>original: *Spiritu Mundi*</note> have revealed themselves within man. All fiery science <note>In this context, "science" (*Scienz*) refers to an active, formative knowledge or a latent potential within the soul.</note> according to heat and cold, and all other qualities in particular—indeed, the properties of all nature were made manifest in him according to both evil and good. For as soon as they ate of the earthly fruit into their bodies, then went

### Chapter 7. The Election of Grace.

the balance <note>original: *Temperatur*</note> fell apart, and the body became manifest in all its properties within the **Spirit of the World** <term>Spirit of the World (*Spiritu Mundi*): The animating soul of the physical universe, which governs natural cycles and the material elements.</term>; then heat and cold fell upon him and forced their way into him; likewise, all the properties of nature, in which the foundation of the creature stands, pressed themselves into him in a state of conflict <note>original: *Wiederwillen*</note>, from which arose sickness and the death of frailty.

6. And with this morsel <note>A reference to the eating of the forbidden fruit in Eden.</note> he died to **God's** kingdom and woke up to the kingdom of nature, and was moved from a state of invulnerability into vulnerability <note>original: *aus der Unleidlichkeit in die Leidlichkeit*; these terms refer to being "impassible" (unable to suffer or be changed by outside forces) versus "passible" (subject to pain and external influence).</note>. In his outer body, he became a beast of all beasts, as the animal image of **God**, wherein the Word of **God** revealed itself in an earthly likeness. Thus, according to the outer body, man became a master and prince of all animals, and yet was himself only an animal—though of a nobler **essence** than a beast—and nevertheless, he possessed the property of an animal within himself.

7. And at this hour, a gate of the dark world was opened within man into **God's** wrath—namely, Hell, or the maw of the devil—just as the realm of **Phantasy** <note>In Böhme’s terminology, "Phantasy" refers to a realm of deceptive images and the disordered imagination of the dark world.</note> became manifest in him. The angry **God** (named so according to the kingdom of darkness) became manifest in him and captured him according to the **Science** of the soul <term>Science (*Scienz*): Not "science" in the modern sense, but an innate, active power or potentiality for knowledge within the soul's nature.</term> in the creature. It is not that the foundation of the soul's **Science** can be broken, but rather the creature made from the **three first principles**—**Salt**, **Sulfur**, and **Mercury** <term>Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury (*Sale, Sulphure und Mercurio*): The alchemical "Tria Prima." Salt represents contraction and stability; Sulfur represents fire and energy; Mercury represents movement and mediation.</term>—as the eternal nature, and also the temporal nature in the **Spirit of the World**. The temporal nature was placed into the earthly property, and the eternal nature into the fury of the dark world, as a neighbor to the devil.

8. Now, when these prisons were to be broken in both natures through the death of Christ, the earth trembled at it, and the sun lost its light; this was to indicate that because the eternal light had now been born again, the temporal light must cease.

9. To properly consider what it was in man that died in the Fall, we must not only look at temporal death—how man dies and decays—for that is only the animal death and not the eternal death. Nor must we be so blind as to say that the soul died in its creaturely state; no, that could not be, for that which is from the Eternal cannot accept death. Rather, it was the **Image of God** <note>original: *Ebenbildniß GOttes*</note> that had [incorporated itself] into the creaturely soul...

# XV. Regarding Chapter 7.

For in God there is no dying, but only a separation of principles, understood in the way we see that night swallows the day, and day the night; thus one is in the other as if dead, for it cannot reveal itself.

10. This is to be understood in a parable: as if the sun were to vanish, then the **Spirit of the World** <term>Spirit of the World (*Spiritus Mundi*): The animating soul of the physical universe, which governs natural cycles and the material elements.</term> would become a vain, raw hostility and an everlasting night; thus the four elements in their current property could not function <note>original: *qualificiren*; in Böhme's sense, this means to interact, produce qualities, or generate life.</note>, and no fruit would grow, nor could any creature live within the four elements. In the same way, Adam and his Eve died to the kingdom of the divine sun-power—which is the divine essence and will—and woke up to the fierce nature within their souls, and also on the outside in the animal-like quality.

11. The soul's **Active Power** <term>Active Power (*Scienß*): Derived from the Latin *scientia*, Böhme uses this to describe the inner "science" or driving force of the will that gives a creature its specific nature.</term> from the **unfathomable will** <term>Unfathomable Will (*ungründlicher Wille*): The "groundless" or bottomless desire that exists before all creation, out of which God generates life.</term>, in which God gives birth, has not died, for nothing can break it; rather, it remains an eternal free will. However, its form as a creature—the soul, which was formed into an image by the Spirit of God—that same image from eternal nature lost the holy **Essence** <term>Essence (*Ens*)</term> in which God’s light and love-fire burned. It is not that this essence became a "nothing"; rather, it became as nothing to the creaturely soul, being unperceivable. Instead, the holy power—the Spirit of God, which was the active life within—hid itself. This was not by its own purpose, but the eternal **Active Power**, as the unfathomable will toward the soulish creature, departed from the will of love into its own thorny property of the soulish nature.

12. God did not withdraw Himself from the soul; rather, the **Active Power** of the free will withdrew itself from God, just as the sun does not withdraw from the thistle, but the thistle withdraws its thorny **Active Power** from the sun and leads it into a thorny essence; the more the sun shines upon it, the thornier and stronger the **Active Power** of the working will becomes; thus it is also to be understood regarding the soul.

13. God dwells through everything, even through the darkness and through the devils, but the darkness does not grasp Him; neither does the devil, nor the godless soul. You say—

### Chapter 7. The Election of Grace.

...why is that? Because the creaturely will toward true, surrendered humility (submitting itself to God’s obedience) is dead; it is only a thistle- and thorn-will in the life of the creature. Thus, this thorn-will keeps the noble **Active Power** <term>Active Power (*Scienz*): The inner "science" or driving force of the will that gives a creature its specific nature.</term> of the **unfathomable** <term>Unfathomable (*ungründlich*): The "groundless" quality of the eternal will.</term> eternal will of the **Abyss** <term>Abyss (*Ungrund*): The "groundless" or "un-grounded" state before all being, where no center or location exists.</term> imprisoned or hidden within itself; they are within each other like day and night.

14. The creaturely soul became as night; the **Spirit of the World** <term>Spirit of the World (*Spiritus Mundi*): The animating soul of the physical universe, which governs natural cycles and the material elements.</term>—which in the beginning stood in a balanced state <note>original: *Temperatur*; a state of harmony where no single quality or element dominates the others.</note> within the body—now stood in both evil and good, as all temporal things do. But the devil’s thistle-seed had entered into it, wherein temporal death lay hidden; there was nothing more to be understood there than a "beast of all beasts." The likeness of the formed, spoken Word stood in enmity and reluctance; the angelic image was entirely destroyed, both in the mind and the senses. As we still see today, the senses always grasp for the animal-like will for self-love and only with great difficulty come to love God and equality. Instead, they constantly soar upward, wanting to possess everything alone and wanting to be the "fairest child in the house," from which pride, greed, envy, and hate arise. All of this is the **Serpent’s Essence** <term>Essence (*Ens*): A fundamental spiritual substance or "being."</term> and the property introduced by the devil, which cannot inherit the Kingdom of God.

15. To this soul, the living, eternally-speaking Word—the property of the highest Love—came to help out of pure grace. It spoke itself again into the faded **Essence** of the heavenly world-being, leading it toward an active life. Just as the devil’s word had spoken itself into the soul, so came the Word of God’s Love and spoke itself back into the faded **Essence**. This was to indicate that there is a goal of an eternal **Covenant of Grace** <term>Covenant of Grace (*Gnaden-Bund*): The divine promise of salvation and reconciliation through God’s mercy.</term>, wherein God’s love, in the name of Jesus, would destroy the devil’s works. It would reintroduce the living, holy **Essence** into the name of Jesus through this "speaking-in" <note>The German *Einsprechen* refers to God's act of breathing or speaking his divine nature back into the human soul.</note> or "in-spoken Word," which occurred in Christ’s incarnation.

So now the creating Word and the created Word were to do it in the power of the Holy Spirit. The heavenly **being** <note>original: *Ens*</note> in the Word, as the temple of the Holy Spirit, was to take upon itself a soulish seed in the woman’s seed, and also a bodily seed from Adam’s essence, from the **slime of the earth** <note>original: *Limo der Erden*, referring to the "dust" or "clay" of the ground from which Adam was formed in Genesis 2:7.</note>, in the same manner as God has taken the world into Himself, yet dwells in heaven in the holy **being** <note>original: *Ente*</note>.

21. Thus the Word took the faded holy **being** from within into its own living **being**, and made the faded one alive in its power. The soulish and bodily nature of the inner (and outer) world remained attached to that same **being** <note>original: *Ente*</note>, just as nature hangs upon God, through which He reveals Himself. Thus, here too, the holy Word wished to reveal itself with the holy **being** through the soulish and bodily nature, and to **tincture** <term>tincture (*tingiren/Tinctur*): An alchemical term used here to describe the spiritual transformation or "coloring" of the soul with divine grace to restore its original purity.</term> the soul again with the highest **tincture**, and to break the devil's established robber-castle within the wrath of eternal nature. All of this was fulfilled in the **Process of Christ** <term>Process of Christ (*Proceß Christi*): The historical and mystical sequence of Christ’s life, suffering, death, and resurrection, viewed as a necessary path for the soul's redemption.</term>.

22. Now tell me here, O Reason: where would there be a place for a pre-determined will of God for the hardening of man?

## XV. Regarding Chapter 7.

Where is the **decree** <note>original: *Vorsatz*, often translated as "purpose" or "divine decree," referring here to the doctrine of predestination.</note>, that He has ordained a crowd in His decree for damnation, and another for eternal life? For sin began in Eve, and grace also began in Eve, before she was even pregnant with a child. They all lay in Eve in the same death, and they also all lay in the single **Covenant of Grace** in life, as the Apostle says: *Romans 5:18*. Just as sin came from one, and spread to all; so also grace came from one, and spread to all. For the Covenant did not apply only to a particular part—to a mere piece of Eve—but to the whole Eve (excluding the devil's works, which he had thrown into her: these Christ was meant to break).

23. No soul could or should be born from the **being** <term>being (*Ente* / *Ens*): A technical term for the fundamental essence or substance of a thing.</term> introduced by the devil, because the Word of God with the Covenant stood in the way: thus the Covenant pressed upon Eve's soul in Adam, as if from the **light-tincture** into Adam's **fiery tincture** <term>tincture (*Tinctur*): In Böhme's thought, a "tincture" is a spiritual or alchemical life-force that gives quality and color to existence; the "fiery" represents the soul's strength, while the "light" represents divine love and clarity.</term>. For Adam and Eve were one human in the Word; thus grace also pressed upon that same single human, Adam and Eve.

24. Where now is the divine eternal decree of which **Reason** <note>Reason (*Vernunft*): Böhme frequently personifies "Reason" as the limited human intellect that tries to understand divine mysteries through logic alone without spiritual rebirth.</note> speaks? It wishes to prove the same with Holy Scripture and yet does not understand it: for the words of Scripture are true, but an understanding is required for them—not an external delusion, where one imagines a foreign God who lives somewhere far away and high in a heaven alone.

25. Brotherly, we wish to indicate to Reason how the Scripture is to be understood when it speaks of the decree and of God's **election** <note>original: *Wahl*</note>, and to give it the true understanding of how the election originates and what the decree might be. We wish to despise no one in or for their held opinion; rather, for the sake of greater knowledge and Christian unity of understanding, we wish to explain the Scripture, for which end this little book was also written.

26. To understand this, we wish to place the first and second **principles** <note>original: *Principium*</note> against one another: namely, the **Kingdom of Nature** (as the realm for divine revelation, wherein God’s wrath and **hardening** <term>hardening (*Verstockung*): The theological concept of obduracy or "the hardening of the heart," often discussed in relation to Pharaoh.</term> are understood) and then the **Kingdom of Grace** (as the true Divine Being). We will see how the ground of hardening originates, and we wish to reconcile the passages of Scripture which seem to be against one another.

<summary>Böhme argues against the doctrine of selective predestination, asserting that because grace was given to Eve before she bore children, it extends to all of humanity. He proposes a "unity of understanding" by looking at the interaction between the Kingdom of Nature (wrath) and the Kingdom of Grace.</summary>

<keywords>predestination, Covenant of Grace, Eve, Adam, Light-tincture, Fiery tincture, Reason, Election, Kingdom of Nature, Kingdom of Grace, hardening, universal grace</keywords>

# Chapter 7. The Election of Grace.

...let us test this, so that each person may see the ground of his own opinion; we do not wish to bind ourselves to any single opinion just to please someone, but rather to demonstrate the foundation to all parties of opinion, in love, for the sake of brotherly unity.

27. When Adam and Eve had fallen, they were blind to the Kingdom of GOD and as if dead; there was no possibility within them to do anything good—understand this as referring to the soulish and bodily creature. However, the <term>Science</term> <note>original: *Scientz*. In Böhme's thought, this refers to a formative, active "knowing" or a spiritual seed that gives rise to manifestation.</note> of the <term>Abyss</term> <note>original: *Ungrund*. This is Böhme’s term for the "Groundless" or the primal "nothingness" that exists before and beyond all created things, out of which the Divine Will arises.</note> out of the Father’s property—in which a soul was formed within the fiery Word—remained unbound, neither evil nor good, for it is the single Will. In this eternal Will, GOD the Father begets His Son; outside of this begetting (as the Divine Power), He is called neither Father nor GOD, but rather the eternal, groundless Will toward "something." In this Will, the birth of the Holy <term>Trinity</term> <note>original: *Dreyheit*</note> as well as the origin of nature and the beginning of all beings are understood.

28. This same Will is the eternal beginning of Divine Wisdom, as the contemplation of the <term>Abyss</term>, and is also the beginning of the Word, as the speaking-forth of the Fire and Light. This "speaking," however, does not occur in the Will of the Abyss, but rather in the comprehension of the <term>Science</term>, where that same Will enters into the "place" of GOD, namely into the Trinity of the begetting. There, the Word of power speaks itself into the distinctness of Science: and in this same distinctness of the speaking Science, the Image of GOD—which is Man—was seen from eternity in Divine Power and Wisdom, in a magical form, without being a creaturely thing. And in this envisioned Image, GOD’S Spirit loved Himself in the highest Love (which is the name JESUS), for it was a figure of His likeness according to power and birth.

29. But because GOD’S Love would not have been manifest without eternal nature—just as the <term>Love-fire</term> would not be manifest without the <term>Wrath-fire</term>—so the root of <term>Science</term> in its ground of nature was the Wrath-fire, and the revelation of the Wrath-fire was the Love-fire, in the same way that light comes from fire; and here we understand the foundation.

# XV. Regarding Chapter 7.

...souls were extinguished, the creaturely soul was only a <term>Source of Torment</term> <note>original: *Quall*. Böhme often uses this word as a pun, blending "Quelle" (source/wellspring) and "Qual" (torment/agony) to describe the nature of the soul separated from God.</note> of **GOD’S** wrath, as a fiery nature. In Böhme's lexicon, this refers to a formative, creative "knowing" or spiritual seed that gives rise to actual existence.</note> of speaking forth (understand this as the human eternal image, into which the creaturely soul was created). And in this incorporation, man has been foreseen in Christ **JESUS** before the foundation of the world. But when the creaturely, natural soul fell and lost the light, the Word of power (which had formed the soul in the fiery science) spoke itself into the will of the <term>Abyss</term> <note>original: *Ungrund*. The "Groundless" or primal nothingness out of which the Divine Will arises.</note> toward the creature.

31. From eternity, the name **JESUS** has stood in an immovable love within man, as in the likeness of **GOD**: for had it been movable, the image would have had a life of its own; but now the true life was alone in the Word of powers (John 1: 4). But when the soul lost the light, the Word spoke the name **JESUS**, in movement, into the faded <term>Essence</term> <note>original: *Ens* (Latin). Refers to the underlying spiritual substance or "being."</note> of the heavenly world's essence.

32. But in this time of need, when the soul fell, **GOD** revealed the riches of His glory and holiness in the groundless will of the soul, as in the eternally-beheld image, and incorporated Himself with the living voice of the Word from the Divine fire of love into the eternal likeness, as a <term>Banner</term> <note>original: *Panier*. A standard or rallying point toward which the soul must strive.</note> for the soul, toward which it should press: and although the soul was capable of no pressing-in (for it was as if dead to **GOD**), yet the Divine Breath pressed into it and exhorted it to cease its wicked activity, so that His voice might again begin to work within the soul.

in **JEHOVA**, revealed in Adam: and now it was revealed in the voice of the <term>In-speaking</term> <note>original: *Einsprechen*. This refers to the divine "Word" speaking itself back into the human soul to begin the process of salvation.</note> in the name of **JESUS**, who had unfolded himself out of **JEHOVA**, with such a covenant, that the name **JESUS** would, in the fullness of time, introduce the holy essence of *Wisdom* <note>original: *Sophiæ* (Latin/Greek). In Böhme’s thought, Sophia is the feminine personification of Divine Wisdom and the "mirror" of God.</note>, as the heavenly holy essence from love—with which love is enclosed, or (as one might put it, wherein the fiery love is an essence)—into the faded essence from **JEHOVA**.

34. But that I say the essence from **JEHOVA** has faded in the Fall, that is true, and is precisely the death in which Adam and Eve died: for they lost the true fire, and there awoke in them the hot and cold fire of enmity, in which fire *Wisdom* is not manifest; for it is not the Divine Fire-Life, but the natural one; and in this natural fire-life of the soul there is now the distinction between **GOD’S** love and wrath.

35. The natural fire-life without the light is **GOD’S** wrath; it desires only its own kind; this hardens the soul and leads it into its own alien will, according to the property of the love-fire. Now, however, it is not as if a free will of a wrath-fire enters into the natural soul to take possession of it; rather, it is the soul's own fire, of which the soul is an essence.

36. The <term>Wrath</term> <note>original: *Grimm*. A key term for Böhme, referring to the fierce, harsh, and foundational energy of nature when it is not tempered by divine light.</note> of its own nature hardens itself, with the inclusion of the <term>Aversion</term> <note>original: *Eckel*. Literally "disgust" or "nausea," used here to describe the clashing, "sickly" state of the natural properties when they are out of balance.</note> in the three first original states of nature (Salt, Sulfur, Mercury <note>original: *Sale, Sulphure, Mercurio* (Latin). These are the three Paracelsian "principles" of alchemy, which Böhme uses to describe the spiritual and physical makeup of all things.</note>), as the property of the dark world, which becomes manifest in the false desire, and then also from outward accidents, which the false lust from the fiery desire grasps within itself; just as Adam and Eve hardened and poisoned themselves with the introduced "serpent-craving," whereupon immediately that same introduced poison also began to hunger after its own kind; then one aversion gave birth to another, as the Apostle Paul says of this in Romans 7:17: that it is not he in the Spirit of Christ who wills and works sin, but the sin in the flesh—that is, that which is in nature as the manifest wrath of eternal and temporal nature—and that which introduces animal lust into the flesh, that is what does it.

<meta>This page continues the investigation into the "three grounds" of human existence. Following the previous page’s conclusion that the inward ground is the "inner heaven" or the Sabbath, this section explores the "Election of Grace" (predestination)—specifically how a soul moves from the fallen state of Adam into the living body of Christ through the indwelling of Divine Wisdom.</meta>

### Chapter 7. Election of Grace.

42. Question. Now the question is: Why are they not all weary and heavy-laden, and why do they not come to refreshment (as to the new birth)? Answer. Thus speaks Christ: John 6:44. No one comes to me, unless my heavenly Father draws him.

43. Question. So now the question is: Which ones does the Father draw to Christ? Answer. The Scripture answers: John 1:13. Those who are born not of the flesh, nor of blood, nor of the will of a man, but of **God**.

44. Question. Which ones are these? Answer. These are those who are born out of Grace; these He chooses for himself.

45. Question. What is Grace? Answer. It is the inward ground, as Christ, who gave himself again as a Grace into the faded inner ground: Those who are now born anew out of that same inward ground, out of <term>Sophia</term> <note>original: *Sophia*. In Böhme's mystical system, Sophia represents Divine Wisdom or the "heavenly virginity"—the pure, spiritual mirror of God that man lost at the Fall and must regain.</note>, as the heavenly virginity, these are members of Christ’s body, and a temple of **God**; these are chosen to be children, the others are hardened, as the Scripture says throughout.

36. Question. <note>The printed text uses '36.' here, though it follows 45 in the numerical sequence.</note> How does it come about that they are hardened? Answer. They have all died in Adam, and without the Grace in Christ, they cannot have or attain the Divine Life.

47. Question. Can the creaturely soul then, by its own power and will, in its <term>selfhood</term> <note>original: *Selbstheit*. This refers to the ego or the "I-will" that operates independently of God's will.</note>, take nothing of Grace? Answer. No, it cannot, for it does not depend on anyone’s self-willing, running, or racing, but on **God's** mercy, Romans 9:16, which is solely in Christ in Grace.

48. Question. Now the question follows: How then does mercy enter the soul, so that it comes under the Election? Answer. As said above, those who are born not of flesh nor blood, nor of the will of man, but of the blessed seed of the woman, as out of the inward ground where the soul draws Christ into itself. Not from an assumed outward Grace, as <term>Reason</term> <note>original: *Vernunft*. Böhme uses this term to describe human logic or scholastic theology that tries to understand God through external rules rather than internal experience.</note> says—that **God** accepts the sinful human being in Christ (who lies dead in sins) through the pre-ordained <term>Election of Grace</term> <note>original: *Gnaden-Wahl*. Predestination.</note>, so that He might make known the riches of His grace. Romans 9:23. No, that does not apply, for the Scripture says, Matthew 18:3. Unless you turn, and become as

It emphasizes that human "Reason" and "self-will" cannot achieve salvation; it must come from the indwelling "inward ground."</summary>

### XV. Of Chapter 7.

48. [become as] children, and you will be reborn through water and Spirit, otherwise you shall not see God's Kingdom. <note>John 3:5.</note> The inward, innate grace of adoption <note>original: *Kindschaft*, the state of being a child of God.</note> alone is valid, for Christ says: What is born of the Spirit is spirit, and what is born of the flesh is flesh. <note>John 3:6.</note> And further: Flesh and blood shall not inherit God's Kingdom. <note>1 Corinthians 15:50.</note>

49. **Question.** Now the question arises: how is this innate childlike birth [possible], seeing as they are all dead in Adam? Must then only some be born and elected as God’s children out of a purpose, and the others remain hardened in God’s purpose? What can the child do if God does not want to have him? **Answer.** Herein lies the nut now to be cracked, which is the cause of the dispute.

50. Christ speaks: A rotten tree cannot bear good fruits, and a good tree cannot bear evil fruits. <note>Matthew 7:18.</note> If we now wish to establish the ground of this, we must establish that same Tree of Knowledge, which is both evil and good, and see what kind of fruits it bears, and from what kind of <term>Essens</term> <note>original: *Essens* or *Ens*. In Böhme's thought, this refers to the spiritual substance or "being" that underlies physical matter.</note> each fruit grows; thus we arrive at the goal, as we then see how each power draws itself into an essence and a will.

51. The Scripture says: God has enclosed all things in number, measure, and weight, <note>Wisdom of Solomon 11:20.</note> as they are meant to go. Now, however, we cannot say of man that he was enclosed in Time in the beginning, for he was enclosed in Eternity in Paradise. God had created him in His image; but when he fell, that same conclusion of Time seized him, wherein all things stand in number, measure, and weight. And that same <term>Clockwork</term> <note>original: *Uhrwerck*. Böhme uses the image of a clock to describe the physical universe (the "outspoken Word") as a complex, pre-programmed machine driven by the planetary wheels and the underlying forces of nature.</note> is the outspoken, formed Word of God according to Love and Wrath, wherein lies the whole Creation together with man, according to nature and the creaturely state.

52. Now in this outspoken Word, the property of the Father—the name **Jesus**—has revealed itself, in that all power in heaven and on earth is given to Him; thus all is His, the evil and the good—not in the possession of His own self-property, but to the good for salvation, and to the evil as a judge. And all things are set against one another, Love against Wrath, and Wrath against Love, so that one may be made manifest in the other until the Day of Separation.

53. This Tree of Knowledge now stands in the highest anxiety <note>In Böhme’s writing, "anxiety" (*Aengstlichkeit*) describes the intense, narrow tension of nature during the process of "birthing" or manifesting spiritual life.</note> in the birth: on one part it is Christ, and on the other part it is the Kingdom of Nature, in the wrath of God the Father, according to the property of the dark and fire-world; the fiery world gives the <term>Ens</term> <note>original: *Ens*. The spiritual substance or "stuff" from which a thing is formed.</note> for the spirit-life; and Christ in His love gives the <term>Ens</term> for the essence of the fruit, and tinctures <note>original: *tingiret*. An alchemical term for transforming or "coloring" a base substance into something higher or divine.</note> the wrath, so that it becomes a kingdom of joy in the Being of all beings.

54. Herein now is the struggle: into which <term>Ens</term> the center of nature—acting as the will of the <term>Ungrund</term> <note>original: *Ungrund*. The "Groundless" or "Abyss"—the primordial freedom or will that exists before and beyond all created nature.</note>—introduces and forms itself within the property of the eternal Father; whether into the grace of Christ in <term>Sophia</term> <note>original: *Sophia*. Divine Wisdom, often personified by Böhme as the eternal, virginal "mirror" or "bride" of the soul.</note>, or into the power of the fierce fire toward Phantasy <note>original: *Phantasey*. In this context, it refers to a state of self-delusion, the false reality or "hellish" imagination of the fallen ego.</note>. Such a form then stands there according to the soul, for here the Father gives the soul to His Son, Christ: for in the Father’s property is the forming of the soul, and in the Son’s property is the noble forming of Sophia, as the eternal virginity in Christ. Now, at this point, it depends on the will of the <term>Ungrund</term> (outside of nature) regarding the soulful creature: to which side it separates itself; either into "Selfhood" as Lucifer did, or into the birthing toward the Holy Trinity of the Deity—that is, whether it lets itself into God, or chooses its own willing, running, and racing.

55. Here now is the choice concerning this, and it is called here as St. Paul says:

> Romans 6:16. To whomsoever you yield yourselves as servants in obedience, his servants you are; whether of sin unto death, or of obedience to God unto righteousness.

56. Thus Reason speaks: What can a child help it, that it becomes a thistle before it even has its life and understanding? **Answer.** Listen: what can God's love in Christ help it, that Adam went out from the <term>Temperature</term> <note>original: *Temperatur*. The state of perfect balance and harmony between the conflicting qualities of nature before the Fall.</note> into the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, into the struggle? For he had a free will; why did he break it himself, against God’s will within him? Why did he become disobedient to God?

57. Thus Reason speaks further: Do all humans then come into the world in such a condition? **Answer.** No, in no way from God’s purpose, but rather from the source of the... <meta>catchword: actualizing- (wirckli-)</meta>

He argues against strict predestination by asserting that the "thistle-like" nature of the wicked stems from Adam's original misuse of free will, not God's decree.</summary>

# XV. Regarding Chapter 7.

actual sins of parents and ancestors: for God speaks in Moses: <note>original: "Exod. 20: 5. 6."</note> Exodus 20:5–6: I will visit and punish the sins of the parents upon the children, unto the third and fourth generation; but to those who love me, I do well, even unto the thousandth generation.

58. Herein now lies the true ground of the **Thistle-Children**, and their hardening; namely, that the parents invite the Devil’s malice into flesh and blood, into the <term>Mystery</term> <note>original: *Mysterium*. In Böhme’s thought, the "Mysterium" is the hidden, spiritual core or "blueprint" that precedes physical manifestation.</note> of the formed, spoken Word of God—such as falsehood, lies, pride, greed, envy, and malice. Often, strong curses are also involved, which for certain reasons are wished into their body and soul by another; and if that person has caused them, then those curses remain in the tree of his life. From this, such branches are born which cannot reach the <term>Ens</term> of Christ <note>original: *Ens Christi*. The spiritual substance or "divine stuff" of Christ that nourishes the soul.</note>, but are born only of the parents' flesh and blood, in the will of the man and woman. There, the soulish <term>Ens</term> <note>original: *Ens*. The essential being or spiritual matter of the soul.</note> introduces itself into a thistle-like nature, often into the property of serpents, dogs, or abominable beasts.

59. And over these Thistle-Children, who on earth neither will nor do anything good, the **Election** passes. And although the parents often still have or retain a spark of the Divine Essence within themselves, and finally enter into repentance for the new birth, yet in the meantime, such Thistle-Children are conceived.

60. There is also a very great difference between those whom the Divine Call seizes in the active Tree of Life; for Christ says: *Many are called, but few are chosen.* <note>original: "Matth. 20: 16."</note> Matthew 20:16. The **Call** is thus to be understood: Christ is the Call; He calls without ceasing in the essence of the Tree: *Come to me, all you who are weary and burdened.* <note>Reference to Matthew 11:28.</note> He stretches out His hand all day long to a disobedient people that will not let itself be drawn, and will not let its spirit be rebuked, as the Scripture laments throughout.

61. Now, the Call goes out over all people; He calls them all; for it stands written: *God wills that all people be helped.* <note>original: "1. Tim. 2: 4."</note> 1 Timothy 2:4. Also: *You are not a God who wills evil.* <note>original: "Pf. 5: 5."</note> Psalm 5:5. God does not will in His own will that even a single Thistle-Child be born

# Chapter 7. The Election of Grace. 95

...[it] will be, but His wrath according to nature seizes them: yet it happens nonetheless that the Divine Call also takes some hold and roots itself, so that in many there is a spark of Christ's **Ens** <term>Ens</term> <note>original: *Ens*. This refers to the essential spiritual substance or "stuff" of Christ's being that can take root in a person.</note>, as if from the Divine hearing of God's voice. God allows these people to be preached to and taught, and reveals His will to them: for they are those who are heavily burdened with sins and lie half-dead at Jericho <note>A reference to the Parable of the Good Samaritan (Luke 10); Böhme uses this to represent the soul wounded by sin and left helpless on the road of life.</note>; for them Christ has ordained Baptism and the Lord’s Supper, and calls out at all times: Come, come, and work in my vineyard, take my yoke upon you, *Matthew 11: 29.* namely the corrupted nature of the formed, spoken Word, which has become a yoke for Christ, wherein the sins of mankind lie.

62. Regarding these, Christ now says: To one was given one pound, to another two, to the third three, to the fourth four, to the fifth five: with these they should trade and acquire much. *Matthew 25: 14, 15.* Now, such a person who has only a tiny spark of God's voice within himself may, if he himself wills, work within it and draw it out into a large tree: for to such He has given the power to become children of God, *John 1: 12.* not by their own will or ability, but by the ability of this little spark: (For the soul rests within it, and the "drawing" of the Father within the soul toward Christ occurs there) for as soon as the soul tastes God’s grace, the Father's will hastens in the unfathomable **Science** <term>Science</term> <note>original: *Scienz*. From the Latin *scientia*, Böhme uses this to mean the inner, active knowledge or the "imaging" power of the mind and will that directs a person's spiritual growth.</note> toward the fountain-spring of Christ. And even if the Kingdom of God is at first small, like a mustard seed; if the soul only accepts it and works within it with its fiery desire, it eventually grows large as a laurel tree.

63. But whatever soul will not accept this, but instead goes into the lusts of the flesh and courts the devil; of those Christ says: To him who has, shall be given; that is, he who works with the little he has, to him shall be given: but he who has not, that is, he who has something but does not want to work within it, from him it shall be taken and given to him who has much. *Matthew 25: 29.* And here it is said: Many are called, but few are chosen. *Matthew 20: 16.*

64. For many of them have the pledge of grace, but they trample it with their feet and do not respect it: one part be-

<meta>catchword: cause</meta>

<summary>Böhme explains that even the "half-dead" sinner has a spark of Christ's essence that can be cultivated. He uses the parables of the talents and the mustard seed to show that spiritual growth depends on the soul's willingness to "work" with the grace it has been given.</summary>

# XV. From Chapter 7. 96
<meta>catchword: and</meta>

[...partly] because of external accidents, and partly because of the coarseness of the **animal-like nature** <term>animal-like nature</term> <note>original: *Viehische Eigenschaft*. Böhme uses this to describe the lower, instinctive, or "beastly" parts of human nature that were corrupted during the Fall, which resist spiritual refinement.</note>. For Christ sows His voice in His word, like a sower sows his seed. It is sown in all people, the ungodly as well as the pious: but now, once the seed is sown, it depends on the quality of the field where the seed falls; if it falls on a hard path, such as an animal-like nature where a coarse beast sits within the fleshly nature, then it is trampled by coarseness and inattentiveness. But if a greedy beast sits within, such as a dog, wolf, or similar nature, then the worries of greed lie in the way and choke the seed. But if it falls into a high-minded spirit that sits in worldly power and honor, then **pride** <term>pride</term> <note>original: *Hoffart*. A term for arrogance or vanity that Böhme sees as a primary spiritual obstacle.</note> has blocked the way; this seed has fallen on a rock and brings forth no fruit. But if it falls into a good reason, where the nature of a human—such as **true humility**—resides, there it is captured, and such a person is a good field: for God’s essence is humility, so this quality is a likeness to Him; there the seed sprouts and bears much fruit.

65. Therefore one should rightly consider the Scripture when it says:

> Many are called, but few are chosen; <note>Matthew 22:14</note>

it understands it thus: Very many, indeed the greatest multitude, are seized by the **Divine Call** <term>Divine Call</term> <note>original: *Göttlicher Ruff*. The internal and external invitation from God to return to a state of grace.</note> and could come to the state of childhood; but their godless life, into which they give themselves and are corrupted by external accidents, hardens them. Therefore a child is often more blessed than an old person, and Christ also says:

> Let the little children come to me, for of such is the kingdom of God; <note>Matthew 19:14</note>

Christ has taken them into His call or covenant. But when a person comes of age and steps out of the Divine Call, surrendering themselves to the devil's will, they nevertheless comfort themselves with an externally assumed **adoption by grace** <term>adoption by grace</term> <note>original: *Gnaden-Kindschaft*. The theological concept of being made a child of God through grace rather than by nature.</note>, as **Babel** <term>Babel</term> <note>original: *Babel*. Böhme’s term for the "city of confusion"—the organized, institutional churches of his time which he believed were more concerned with dogma and power than true spiritual rebirth.</note> does, saying:

> "Oh, Christ has done it; He has paid the price. I only need to comfort myself with this and accept it; His grace will be credited to me as a gift. I will be saved in **God’s purpose** <term>God’s purpose</term> <note>original: *GOttes Vorsatz*. Referring here to the doctrine of predestination or the divine decree of election.</note> without any works of my own will. I am indeed dead in sins and can do nothing good without Him, unless He draws me into it; but He will make His purpose known in me and make me a child of grace through His external acceptance of me."

<summary>Böhme uses the Parable of the Sower to explain that while God's word is offered to everyone, it only flourishes in those who possess "true humility." He critiques the "Babel" of organized religion for teaching a passive view of salvation that relies on external grace without internal transformation.</summary>

### Chapter 7. The Election of Grace.

...and forgive me my sin: even though I live wickedly, I am still a **Child of Grace** <term>Child of Grace</term> <note>original: *Gnaden-Kind*. A person chosen by God's favor. Böhme is critiquing the idea that one can live immorally and still rely on being "predestined" for heaven.</note> in His purpose.

66. Of these the Scripture says, Psalm 69:23: *Let their way become a snare and a pitfall.* Also: He lets their light go out in the midst of darkness and hardens them in their own delusion, for their ways are harmful. The **election** <term>election</term> <note>original: *Wahl*. The theological concept of God choosing certain individuals for salvation.</note> passes over these people, for they were called from the beginning and are still called at all times, but they do not want to come.

67. Thus Christ speaks: *We played the flute for you, and you did not dance:* Matthew 11:17. Also: *O Jerusalem, how often I wanted to gather your children together, as a hen gathers her chicks under her wings, and you yourself were not willing:* Matthew 23:37. You were caught in the **Call of God** <term>Call of God</term> <note>original: *Ruffe GOttes*. The internal and external invitation from God to return to a state of grace.</note>, and you turned yourself away from it by your **Own Will** <term>Own Will</term> <note>original: *Eigenen Willen*. In Böhme’s thought, the "self-will" is the root of evil, where a creature centers its desire on itself rather than on God's universal love.</note>.

68. Then **Reason** <term>Reason</term> <note>original: *Vernunft*. Often used by Böhme to represent human logic or intellectualizing that lacks spiritual illumination.</note> says: *they were not able to.* Answer: Why were they not able, since they were indeed called? He "cannot" who is not included in the call; but who can say who that might be? The Devil within them does not want to; he tears the Word from their hearts so that they do not believe or find salvation, as Christ says; therefore they are rejected in the election. For the election passes over them at the harvest-time, when the plant is ripe and the iniquity is full in its measure: then, when the winnowing happens, the **chaff** <term>chaff</term> <note>original: *Spreu*. The husks of grain; a common biblical metaphor for those who lack spiritual substance.</note>—which is too light in weight—remains behind.

69. It happens as Christ says: *The Kingdom of Heaven is like a sower who sows good wheat, then the enemy comes and sows weeds into it;* Matthew 13:25. and when the weeds grow up, they stifle the wheat so that it cannot grow and bear fruit: so it is also with the human being; many a soul is a good little grain, but the Devil's weeds destroy it.

70. Do you say: *That cannot be, since Christ says, John 10:28: "My sheep are in my hands, no one can snatch them from me"?* Answer: This is all true; but take note: as long as the will of the soul remains in God, the Devil cannot snatch it away; but if the soul breaks itself off from the will of God, then the **insight of the groundless will** <term>insight of the groundless will</term> <note>original: *Scienz des ungründlichen Willens*. "Scienz" (from Latin *scientia*) refers to a living, internal knowledge or spiritual "science." The "groundless will" is the *Ungrund*, the deep, mysterious origin of God's being that is beyond all comprehension.</note> (in which Christ dwells) is darkened, and Christ is crucified in His members and kil-

### Summaries.

The author wishes to present the high mysteries in an image. §. 1
As the seed, so the fruit. 2. 3. How the innermost poured itself into the outermost? 4. In this birth, three **Principles** <term>Principles</term> <note>original: *Principia*. The three foundational levels of existence: the dark fire-world, the light world of God, and the physical world.</note> are understood, 5. as: the fiery, the nature-light-like, and the feeling life. 6. In the 1st, as in the fire, nature is understood; in the 2nd, the light-life; and the 3rd **Principle** has its beginning from the power of fire and light. 7. Good and evil lie in every thing, and it is good; but when it leads itself apart, it becomes a **Contrary** <term>Contrary</term> <note>original: *Contrarium*. A state of conflict where forces work against each other rather than in harmony.</note>. 8. In one kernel lies the whole tree along with the root. 9. Now, if the earth is similar to the kernel in its quality, they accept each other willingly: 10. but if they are unequal, the kernel perishes, or at least stands in great danger. 11. For reluctance soon arises in the **Three First Properties** <term>Three First Properties</term> <note>original: *3 Ersten*. The first three "qualities" of nature—contraction, friction, and anxiety—which form the basis of material existence.</note> of the Mother. 12. In this conflict, the fire ignites in the **Essence** <term>Essence</term> <note>original: *Ens*. The internal spiritual substance or "being" of a thing.</note> of the seed through the power of the sun. 13. For the sun loves every plant and gives to all its love-will 14. just as it is generally a good to the good and a poison to the evil; 15. namely, if the root is of the same will as the earth, the sun gives **Growth** <term>Growth</term> <note>original: *Wachsthum*.</note>: but if they are **opposed** <note>original: *contrair*.</note>, it burns it up. If the Three First can have the juice of the earth, they remain in equality and also harmonize with the sun's light-rays. *ibid.* One also sees in the growth of the branches how, with the rising of the trunk, the conflict also rises, 16. where often a branch [grows] large

# Chapter 8. Election of Grace. 99

[the branch] grows, and the other withers; which happens when they leave their state of equality and break out in pride above the others in the power of fire; <note>original: "Feuers-Macht"</note> 17. there they wither. [In the same place:] <note>original: "ibid."</note> But those who stand in the **Will of the Sun** <note>The "Will of the Sun" represents a life aligned with divine light and natural harmony.</note> grow and become large; although many branches also perish from external accidents caused by the stars. <note>original: "Gestirne". In this context, Böhme refers to the astrological and physical influences of the heavens on earthly life.</note> 18. Thus it also goes with the growth of man. 19. God’s purpose is to bring forth His eternal-speaking Word, 20. for His self-revelation; and the Word’s purpose is the **science of separability**. <term>Science of Separability</term> <note>original: "Scienz der Schiedlichkeit". This refers to the capacity of the unified divine essence to divide into distinct, individual forms and qualities.</note> 21. The Word desires to reveal its power, and the **Great Mystery** <term>Mysterium Magnum</term> <note>original: "Mysterium M." The fundamental, chaotic, or unmanifested state of all potentiality in God.</note> is the eternal essential Word of God. 22. The spiritual sounding Word has expressed itself through the **Great Mystery** into a formality, and the separability is the **Spirit of the World**. <term>Spiritus Mundi</term> <note>original: "Sp. M." The animating soul of the physical universe.</note> 23. This **Great Mystery** has separated itself through the speaking of the Word into the subtleness of the stars, and into that which is coagulated, such as earth, stones, and metals; thus there are also two kinds of properties within the earth itself. 24. The external is now the **Spirit of the World** from the inner Word of God, from which the creation of the external world proceeded, 25. and in this external Mystery the properties stand in struggle, just as they do in the elements. 26. Into that same external Mystery, God has spoken the **Light of Nature** <note>original: "Licht der Natur". The inherent wisdom or guiding principle found within the physical world.</note> from the **Great Mystery** through the power of the eternal Light, 27. and gave the **Sun** as an active life into the external; though both lights <note>The "Light of Nature" and the "Eternal Light."</note> surrender themselves in animals and plants: nevertheless, the **Wrath** <note>original: "Grimm". The harsh, consuming, or "dark" side of God's power that drives nature's fierce competition.</note> still holds all creatures in its power. 28. For God’s purpose is to reveal Himself with light and darkness; but He has given the light to all things as a **temperament**. <term>Temperament</term> <note>original: "Temperatur". A state of balance where conflicting qualities—like heat and cold, or light and dark—are harmonized.</note> 29. Therefore, no creature can complain against its Creator. 30. But the **Wrath** hinders the power of the light, and the curse hinders the holy **tincture** <term>Tincture</term> <note>original: "h. Tinctur". An alchemical term for the spiritual essence or "medicine" that can transform and perfect base matter.</note> of the speaking Word. [In the same place:] <note>original: "ibid."</note> The other purpose of God is the name **JESUS**, which God spoke back into the **Seed of the Woman**. <note>A biblical reference to the promised savior in Genesis 3:15.</note> 31. The first purpose is the property of the Father, just as the second is the name **JESUS**. 32. God spoke this name **JESUS** into the mother of all humans, so that they all, along with the curse, also bring the **Covenant of Grace** with them into the world. 33. In such a way has God revealed the nature of separability through His Word, in which the purposes of wickedness originate, as the "science" <note>active consciousness</note> introduces itself into fire, light, or fantasy. The light is good; the power is in the fire; and in the separability is the **tincture of the Suns**. 34. 35. Man has been brought into an image out of the speaking and spoken word, so that according to the inner spirit, he is of the property of fire and light. 36. The property of light was manifest in him, before the Fall, in **JEHOVA**: after the Fall it is in the holy **JESUS**, 37. and this grace now stands in the soul and calls to it, 37. that it should stand still from the imagery of the earthly creation, so that this purpose may reveal itself with the holy light from the **Love-fire**. <term>Love-fire</term> <note>original: "Liebe-Feuer". The divine heat of God's love, which contrasts with the "consuming fire" of His wrath.</note> 38. This **Word of Grace** lies in the inner ground of all people, and offers itself to every soul. 39. Conversely, God’s **Wrath** also stands in every soul, 40. and moreover, every seed stands according to the external world in the power of the **Spirit of the World**, which fashions an animal according to its **constellation**, <term>Constellation</term> <note>original: "Constellation". The alignment of the stars at birth, believed to shape a person's natural temperament.</note> 41. and as the stars stand in their wheel, such an image he makes. 42. # XV. From Chapter 8. 100
